The counts are computed from the file examined and never hardcoded.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `rows` is the number of data records, that is, every record after the header. It is the same for every column.\n- `nulls` is the number of values in the column for which `internal/profile.IsNull` is true.\n- `distinct` is the number of different non-null values, compared as exact trimmed strings. Case is significant: `true` and `TRUE` are two values. Null values are never counted.\n- A file with a header and no records yields `rows` 0, `nulls` 0, and `distinct` 0 for every column.\n\n## Interface\n\n- Package `internal/profile` exposes a function that takes a column's trimmed values and returns the three counts.\n- The project test suite in `internal/profile` carries tests named `TestCountsRows`, `TestCountsNulls`, `TestCountsDistinct`, and `TestCountsHeaderOnly`, written by the implementer against real output.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC counts-rows ===\nIntent: The row count equals the number of data records, including a header-only input.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestCountsRows\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC counts-rows ===\n\n=== AC counts-nulls ===\nIntent: Empty, NA, N/A, and NULL values are counted as nulls and nothing else is.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestCountsNulls\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC counts-nulls ===\n\n=== AC counts-distinct ===\nIntent: Distinct counts different non-null trimmed strings with case significant and nulls excluded.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestCountsDistinct\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC counts-distinct ===\n\n=== AC counts-header-only ===\nIntent: A header-only input yields zero for all three counts.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestCountsHeaderOnly\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC counts-header-only ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Counts are computed from the file examined; no count is hardcoded.\n- Distinct comparison uses exact trimmed strings, never case folding or numeric equality.\n- Null values are never counted as distinct values.\n\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-MinMax.md ===\n# FEATURE: MinMax\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Report the minimum and maximum for every column type. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-TypeDate.md, FEATURE-TypeOther.md |\n| Provides    | min, max |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile.InferType |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive every column a `min` and `max` appropriate to its inferred type.\n\n## Behavior\n\nStatistics consider non-null values only.\n\n| Type | min | max |\n|---|---|---|\n| `integer` | smallest value, written as a plain integer | largest value, written as a plain integer |\n| `number` | smallest value, exactly two decimals | largest value, exactly two decimals |\n| `date` | earliest date | latest date |\n| `boolean` | `false` if any value is false, else `true` | `true` if any value is true, else `false` |\n| `text` | smallest by byte value | largest by byte value |\n| `empty` | empty string | empty string |\n\n- Integer and number comparison is numeric and exact, using `math/big`. Values such as `-3` and `12` compare as numbers, not strings.\n- Boolean values compare ignoring case, and the written result is lowercase.\n- Number output has a leading `-` when negative, no SumMean\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Compute the exact sum and mean of numeric columns, rounded once half away from zero. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-TypeNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | sum, mean |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile.InferType |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive `integer` and `number` columns an exact `sum` and `mean`, free of floating point error.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Arithmetic uses `math/big` (`big.Rat`) over non-null values. Rounding happens once, when writing.\n- `integer` sum is the integer total written as a plain integer.\n- `number` sum is written with exactly two decimal places.\n- `mean` for `integer` and `number` is the total divided by the non-null count, written with exactly two decimals.\n- Rounding is half away from zero from the exact value. A negative result has a leading `-`; there is no thousands separator.\n- A numeric column with no non-null values is typed `empty`, so it never reaches sum or mean.\n- For every other type `sum` and `mean` are empty strings.\n\n## Interface\n\n- Package `internal/profile` exposes a formatter that renders a `big.Rat` with two decimals, rounded half away from zero, and computes sum and mean strings for a typed column.\n- The project test suite in `internal/profile` carries tests named `TestSumInteger`, `TestSumNumber`, `TestMeanRounding`, `TestMeanNegative`, and `TestSumMeanNonNumeric`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC sum-integer ===\nIntent: Integer sum is the exact total written as a plain integer.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestSumInteger\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC sum-integer ===\n\n=== AC sum-number ===\nIntent: Number sum is exact and written with two decimals.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestSumNumber\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC sum-number ===\n\n=== AC mean-rounding ===\nIntent: Mean is total over non-null count, rounded once half away from zero to two decimals.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMeanRounding\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC mean-rounding ===\n\n=== AC mean-negative ===\nIntent: A negative mean keeps its sign and rounds half away from zero.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMeanNegative\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC mean-negative ===\n\n=== AC summean-non-numeric ===\nIntent: Sum and mean are blank for date, boolean, text, and empty columns.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestSumMeanNonNumeric\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summean-non-numeric ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- No floating point arithmetic is used for sums, means, or rounding.\n- Rounding happens exactly once, at write time.\n- No statistic is hardcoded; every value is computed from the file examined.\n\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Median.md ===\n# FEATURE: Median\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Compute the exact median of numeric columns. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-TypeNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | median |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile.InferType |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive `integer` and `number` columns an exact `median`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Sort the non-null values exactly (`big.Rat`), then take the middle value; when the count is even, take the exact mean of the two middle values.\n- The result is written with exactly two decimal places, rounded once half away from zero, with a leading `-` when negative and no thousands separator. This applies to `integer` columns too.\n- For every non-numeric type `median` is an empty string.\n\n## Interface\n\n- Package `internal/profile` computes the median string for a typed column.\n- The project test suite in `internal/profile` carries tests named `TestMedianOdd`, `TestMedianEven`, `TestMedianUnsorted`, `TestMedianNumber`, and `TestMedianNonNumeric`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC median-odd ===\nIntent: With an odd count the median is the middle sorted value.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMedianOdd\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC median-odd ===\n\n=== AC median-even ===\nIntent: With an even count the median is the exact mean of the two middle values.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMedianEven\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC median-even ===\n\n=== AC median-unsorted ===\nIntent: Input order does not affect the median and the sort is numeric, not lexical.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMedianUnsorted\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC median-unsorted ===\n\n=== AC median-number ===\nIntent: A number median is written with two decimals rounded half away from zero.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMedianNumber\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC median-number ===\n\n=== AC median-non-numeric ===\nIntent: Median is blank for non-numeric columns.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestMedianNonNumeric\")\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC median-non-numeric ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Sorting and averaging are exact; no floating point is used.\n- Null values never take part in the median.\n- No median value is hardcoded.\n\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ProfileOutput.md ===\n# FEATURE: ProfileOutput\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Write the profile table with one row per column in file order. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Counts.md, FEATURE-MinMax.md, FEATURE-SumMean.md, FEATURE-Median.md, FEATURE-TypeDate.md, FEATURE-TypeOther.md |\n| Provides    | profile table on standard output |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAssemble the per-column type and statistics into the CSV table the command writes when profiling a file.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- With a file argument and no `--freq`, write the header `column,type,rows,nulls,distinct,min,max,sum,mean,median`.\n- Then write one row per column in file order, using the trimmed header name.\n- Output uses `encoding/csv`'s writer with `\\n` line endings, so a field containing a comma, a double quote, or a newline is quoted per RFC 4180.\n- The command exits 0 on success.\n- On any failure nothing is written to standard output; the table is built fully before the first byte is written.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `internal/cli.Run` Provides    | profile table on standard output |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAssemble the per-column type and statistics into the CSV table the command writes when profiling a file.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- With a file argument and no `--freq`, write the header `column,type,rows,nulls,distinct,min,max,sum,mean,median`.\n- Then write one row per column in file order, using the trimmed header name.\n- Output uses `encoding/csv`'s writer with `\\n` line endings, so a field containing a comma, a double quote, or a newline is quoted per RFC 4180.\n- The command exits 0 on success.\n- On any failure nothing is written to standard output; the table is built fully before the first byte is written.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `internal/cli.Run` calls the reader, null classifier, type inference, and the statistics, then writes the table to its `stdout` writer.\n- The project test suite carries `TestProfileHeader`, `TestProfileRowOrder`, and `TestProfileQuoting` in `internal/cli`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC profile-columns-and-stats ===\nIntent: Profiling a generated file yields one row per column in file order with counts, types, and statistics matching values computed from that file's own inputs.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport math\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom fractions import Fraction\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\n\ndef fmt(fr):\n    n = math.floor(abs(fr) * 100 + Fraction(1, 2))\n    s = f\"{n // 100}.{n % 100:02d}\"\n    return \"-\" + s if fr < 0 and n != 0 else s\n\n\nheader = [\"id\", \"amt\", \"when\", \"flag\", \"word\", \"gone\"]\nrecords = [\n    [\"3\", \"1.50\", \"2025-01-05\", \"true\", \"pear\", \"NA\"],\n    [\"1\", \"-2.25\", \"2024-12-31\", \"FALSE\", \"apple\", \"\"],\n    [\"2\", \"10.00\", \"2025-03-01\", \"true\", \"pear\", \"null\"],\n    [\"10\", \"N/A\", \"2025-02-14\", \"true\", \"fig\", \"NULL\"],\n    [\"7\", \"4.75\", \"NA\", \"false\", \"apple\", \"N/A\"],\n]\ncols = list(zip(*records))\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n        w = csv.writer(fh)\n        w.writerow(header)\n        w.writerows(records)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0][0] == \"column\"\nassert [r[0] for r in rows[1:]] == header\n\nnulls = {\"\", \"na\", \"n/a\", \"null\"}\n\n\ndef nonnull(col):\n    return [v for v in col if v.strip().lower() not in nulls]\n\n\nby = {r[0]: r for r in rows[1:]}\n\nids = [Fraction(int(v)) for v in nonnull(cols[0])]\nr = by[\"id\"]\nassert r[1] == \"integer\"\nassert r[2] == str(len(records))\nassert r[3] == str(len(cols[0]) - len(ids))\nassert r[4] == str(len(set(nonnull(cols[0]))))\nassert r[5] == str(int(min(ids)))\nassert r[6] == str(int(max(ids)))\nassert r[7] == str(int(sum(ids)))\nassert r[8] == fmt(sum(ids) / len(ids))\ns = sorted(ids)\nmid = len(s) // 2\nmed = s[mid] if len(s) % 2 else (s[mid - 1] + s[mid]) / 2\nassert r[9] == fmt(med)\n\namts = [Fraction(v) for v in nonnull(cols[1])]\nr = by[\"amt\"]\nassert r[1] == \"number\"\nassert r[3] == str(len(cols[1]) - len(amts))\nassert r[5] == fmt(min(amts))\nassert r[6] == fmt(max(amts))\nassert r[7] == fmt(sum(amts))\nassert r[8] == fmt(sum(amts) / len(amts))\ns = sorted(amts)\nmid = len(s) // 2\nmed = s[mid] if len(s) % 2 else (s[mid - 1] + s[mid]) / 2\nassert r[9] == fmt(med)\n\ndates = nonnull(cols[2])\nr = by[\"when\"]\nassert r[1] == \"date\"\nassert r[3] == str(len(cols[2]) - len(dates))\nassert r[5] == min(dates)\nassert r[6] == max(dates)\nassert r[7:10] == [\"\", \"\", \"\"]\n\nr = by[\"flag\"]\nassert r[1] == \"boolean\"\nassert r[4] == str(len(set(cols[3])))\nassert r[5] == \"false\"\nassert r[6] == \"true\"\nassert r[7:10] == [\"\", \"\", \"\"]\n\nwords = nonnull(cols[4])\nr = by[\"word\"]\nassert r[1] == \"text\"\nassert r[5] == min(words, key=lambda v: v.encode())\nassert r[6] == max(words, key=lambda v: v.encode())\nassert r[7:10] == [\"\", \"\", \"\"]\n\nr = by[\"gone\"]\nassert r[1] == \"empty\"\nassert r[2] == str(len(records))\nassert r[3] == str(len(records))\nassert r[4] == \"0\"\nassert r[5:10] == [\"\", \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\"]\n=== END AC profile-columns-and-stats ===\n\n=== AC profile-quoting-and-trim ===\nIntent: A value containing a comma is quoted on output, and padding spaces in header names and values are trimmed.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nname = \"label\"\nvalue = \"red, green\"\nraw = f\"  {name}  ,n\\n  {value}  ,1\\n{value},2\\n\"\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n        fh.write(raw.replace(value, '\"' + value + '\"'))\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nassert out.endswith(\"\\n\")\nassert \"\\r\" not in out\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nby = {r[0]: r for r in rows[1:]}\nassert name in by\nassert by[name][1] == \"text\"\nassert by[name][4] == \"1\"\nassert by[name][5] == value\nassert by[name][6] == value\n=== END AC profile-quoting-and-trim ===\n\n=== AC profile-header-only ===\nIntent: A header-only file reports every column as empty with zero counts and exits 0.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nheader = [\"sku\", \"price\", \"in_stock\"]\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"ragged.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n        fh.write(\"a,b\\n1,2\\n3,4,5\\n\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 3, (code, out, err)\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC
